以下事件由[法律颁布过程](Laws.md)中的推进、辩论或停滞检查点触发。

## 通用法律颁布事件

这些事件可在任何国家颁布给定法律时触发。它们有一些共同点：

- 所有事件都有 5 年的冷却时间，除非某个检查点没有其他可用事件
- 如果相关的利益集团和人物不存在或不满足给定条件，则不会触发
- 如果法律颁布被取消，事件本身也会被取消
- 任何立法修正（enactment modifier）效果都会持续到该法律颁布完成或被取消为止

| 事件 | 正在颁布的法律 | 事件类型 | 其他条件 | 相关利益集团与人物 | 选项 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 行动宣传（Propaganda of the Deed） anarchy.1 无政府主义者宣称对一起绅士俱乐部爆炸案负责，该俱乐部常有[target]成员光顾。[target leader]的遗体在废墟中被发现。“战争，阶级战争，而你们是最先在你们称之为秩序的强大机构的庇护下、在你们法律的黑暗中发动战争的人。必然会有流血；我们不会退缩；必然会有杀戮：我们会杀人，因为这是必要的；必然会有毁灭；我们会毁灭，以将世界从你们暴虐的制度中解放出来。” | 无政府状态（Anarchy） | 停滞 | 未拥有无政府主义恐怖行动（Anarchist Terrorism） 无政府主义恐怖行动 立法修正 −10% 立法成功率 | Target：随机的非边缘化利益集团，其领袖为反动派、家长式、贵族派、卡洛斯派（Carlist）卡洛斯派或卡洛斯派（现代），或温和派且领袖可用 | 这些反动分子罪有应得。 启用条件：法律颁布挫败次数 < 最大挫败次数 - 1 [Target]获得无政府主义暗杀（Anarchist Assassination） 无政府主义暗杀 利益集团修正 −4 利益集团支持度 −20% 利益集团政治力量 获得无政府主义暗杀（Anarchist Assassination） 无政府主义暗杀 国家修正 −3 反对派利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 [Target leader]死亡 我们不能纵容恐怖主义。 获得无政府主义恐怖行动（Anarchist Terrorism） 无政府主义恐怖行动 立法修正 −20% 立法成功率 [Target]获得无政府主义暗杀（Anarchist Assassination） 无政府主义暗杀 利益集团修正 −2 利益集团支持度 −10% 利益集团政治力量 [Target leader]死亡 |

| 无声的呐喊（The Invisible Cry） children_rights_events.1 由于儿童在工作场所面临的不人道条件，[capital state]的许多家庭选择把孩子藏起来，希望他们不会被送去工作。“他们叫我们保持安静，先生，所以我们就照做了。我和我兄弟站在那里，安静得像座坟墓，您瞧，我拼命屏住呼吸，但天太黑了，我以为他们已经走了。他们不会在这儿找到我们的，您瞧，妈妈是这么说的，可是天太黑了，先生，而且很冷。” | 更进步的儿童权利法律： 若拥有允许童工（Child Labor Allowed）：限制童工（Restricted Child Labor） 义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） 若拥有限制童工（Restricted Child Labor）：义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） | 推进 | 未拥有藏匿儿童（Hidden Children） 藏匿儿童 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 | Supporters：随机的非边缘化利益集团，对当前颁布法律的立场 < | 孩子？什么孩子？ 获得藏匿儿童（Hidden Children） 藏匿儿童 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 如果他们藏进那些废弃的房子里，那可就遗憾了…… 获得藏匿儿童（Hidden Children） 藏匿儿童 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 [Supporters]获得协助藏匿儿童（Helped Hide Children） 协助藏匿儿童 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度 −20% 利益集团人口吸引力，持续 2.5 年 |
| 苦涩的真相（A Sour Truth） children_rights_events.2 鉴于[child labor state]工人的工资微薄，越来越多的父母被迫送孩子去工作以维持家庭生计。“这简直是疯了！如果这些人自己强迫孩子去工作，我们还怎么帮助他们？！我们必须做点什么！立即制止这种野蛮的决定！”“呃，显然如果他们有其他选择，这不会是他们愿意让自己的孩子承受的事情，对吧？以生存抉择来评判这些可怜的人也无济于事。” | 更进步的儿童权利法律： 若拥有允许童工（Child Labor Allowed）：限制童工（Restricted Child Labor） 义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） 若拥有限制童工（Restricted Child Labor）：义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） | 辩论 | 任意人口生活水平 <= 10 未拥有童工辩论（Child Labor Debate） 童工辩论 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 未拥有童工让步（Child Labor Concessions） 童工让步 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度 | Opponents：随机的非边缘化利益集团，对当前颁布法律的立场 > Supporters：随机的非边缘化利益集团，对当前颁布法律的立场 <= Child labor state：任意的、存在生活水平 <= 10 的人口的州 | 我们必须制止此事。 50% 概率：获得童工辩论（Child Labor Debate） 童工辩论 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 50% 概率：获得童工辩论（Child Labor Debate） 童工辩论 立法修正 +20% 立法成功率 [Supporters]获得无童工让步（No Child Labor Concessions） 无童工让步 利益集团修正 −2 利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 + [child labor state]中 10% 的下层阶层人口变得更加激进 他们也是不得已而为之。 50% 概率：获得童工辩论（Child Labor Debate） 童工辩论 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 50% 概率：无 [Opponents]获得童工让步（Child Labor Concessions） 童工让步 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 |
| 无法避免的伤害（Unavoidable Injuries） children_rights_events.3 随着政府推行[current law]，成年男性被迫填补儿童留下的空缺。然而，他们的身躯太大、手指太粗，工伤事故不断增加。“他们的惨叫充斥着工厂，他们的鲜血覆盖了机器，仿佛机器也有自己的心脏。这已经是一周内第十次有男人在操作那些以前只有孩子纤细小手才能操控的微型机械时割伤手指。抗议声日益高涨：‘如果不是他们，那就是我们。’” | 更进步的儿童权利法律： 若拥有允许童工（Child Labor Allowed）：限制童工（Restricted Child Labor） 义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） 若拥有限制童工（Restricted Child Labor）：义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） | 停滞 | 未颁布工人保护（Workers' Protections）或监管机构（Regulatory Bodies） 未拥有安抚工人（Appeased the Workers） 安抚工人 立法修正 −20% 立法速度 −10% 立法成功率 未拥有对抗工人（Confronted the Workers） 对抗工人 立法修正 −20% 立法速度 +10% 立法成功率 |  | 谁都不该做这种工作。 获得安抚工人（Appeased the Workers） 安抚工人 立法修正 −20% 立法速度 −10% 立法成功率 孩子们回不来了，他们最好习惯这一点。 获得对抗工人（Confronted the Workers） 对抗工人 立法修正 −20% 立法速度 +10% 立法成功率 + [capital state]中 10% 的劳工变得更加激进 + [capital state]中 10% 的机械师变得更加激进 |
| 慈善学校（Ragged Schools） children_rights_events.4 在我们迈向义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School）的过程中，全国各地都在热烈建议设立慈善学校。这些慈善组织将为社会最贫困的儿童提供食物和教育。“这些既没有人脉也没有家庭的可怜孩子，只有一条路可以在这个社会中立足：教育。如果他们不会写自己的名字，我们就教他们签名；如果他们不会阅读，我们就教他们获取信息；如果这样的孩子没有饭吃，我们就给他找份工作。” | 义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） | 推进 | 已颁布允许童工（Child Labor Allowed）或限制童工（Restricted Child Labor） 未拥有宗教慈善学校（Religious Ragged Schools） 宗教慈善学校 利益集团修正 +1 利益集团支持度 未拥有拒绝慈善学校（Rejected Ragged Schools） 拒绝慈善学校 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度 虔信派未被边缘化 知识分子未被边缘化 未拥有慈善学校（Ragged Schools）类型的修正案 |  | 如此虔诚之事必须交由虔信派处理。 获得慈善学校（Ragged Schools） 慈善学校 立法修正 +10% 立法成功率 由虔信派赞助的慈善学校（Ragged Schools）被加入义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） 虔信派获得宗教慈善学校（Religious Ragged Schools） 宗教慈善学校 利益集团修正 +1 利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 这是一项绝妙的倡议，应当交由教师处理。 虔信派获得拒绝慈善学校（Rejected Ragged Schools） 拒绝慈善学校 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 知识分子获得拒绝慈善学校（Rejected Ragged Schools） 拒绝慈善学校 利益集团修正 −1 利益集团支持度，持续 5 年 |
| 纸与笔（Pen and Paper） children_rights_events.5 由于一些公众抗议，[current law]的颁布陷入停顿。许多家庭缺乏购买力，无法承担购置新学习用品的开支，从而阻碍了子女的入学。“听着，没有任何父母会试图阻止自己的孩子获得比在这里更好的未来，但如果我必须做出决定，而这决定是在买面包和买纸笔之间做选择，那我说这不算一个公平的决定。” | 义务小学教育（Compulsory Primary School） 公立学校（Public Schools） 宗教学校（Religious Schools） | 停滞 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_child_labor_allowed has_law = law_type:law_restricted_child_labor has_law = law_type:law_no_schools }NOT = { has_modifier = school_supplies_modifier }NOT = { has_modifier = no_school_supplies_modifier }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { OR = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_provided_school_supplies has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_unfunded_school_supplies } } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_capital = yes } save_scope_as = child_labor_state } random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes } save_scope_as = school_supplies_ig } | 财富不应成为阻碍因素。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = school_supplies_modifier }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_provided_school_supplies sponsor = scope:school_supplies_ig cooldown = 60 } } 我们无法再投入更多公共资金。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = no_school_supplies_modifier }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_unfunded_school_supplies sponsor = scope:school_supplies_ig cooldown = 60 } } |

| 职业军队 conscription_laws.2 我们的许多职业军人提出了担忧：战时将应征兵员与正规步兵混编，将大大影响部队的素质与作战能力。“你指望我去为这个国家战斗、把命押上，却还要我跟某个连步枪都没摸过的农民或工厂工人并肩作战？当只有一部分人知道自己在做什么时，我们怎么能指望我们的部队能够协调一致地行动？” | 大规模征兵 农民征召 | 辩论 | has_law = law_type:law_professional_army OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies is_enacting_law = law_type:law_mass_conscription }NOT = { has_modifier = conscription_debate } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_conscription } | 我们需要尽可能多的士兵。 scope:armed_forces_conscription = { add_modifier = { name = more_soldiers days = normal_modifier_time } }random_list = { 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 3 } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 2 } } } 我们应当慎重考虑。 add_modifier = { name = listen_to_the_troops days = normal_modifier_time }add_loyalists = { pop_type = soldiers value = 0.1 }add_loyalists = { pop_type = officers value = 0.1 }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 5 } } 4 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = conscription_debate multiplier = 1 } } } |

| 全国工会 corporate_state.1 工会中的部分成员已发声支持官方承认劳工在我国中的地位。他们是制衡共产主义反对派的宝贵力量。"如今资产阶级本身对工人阶级的工会化表现出一种同情和倾向，这绝非不合逻辑。确实，资产阶级中较有头脑的阶层会乐于接受对国家和代议机构的改革，以便给'非政治性'工会更大的空间，甚至接受它们对生产体系实行控制的要求。资产阶级感到，只要无产阶级的行动局限于逐行业提出的眼前经济诉求，这就有助于维护现状，避免形成危险的'政治'意识……" | 社团主义国家 工会社团化 社会君主制 | 推进 | `OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { leader = { NOR = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist has_ideology = ideology:ideology_radical } } ig_counts_as_marginal = no ig_approval > 0 }` | `ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } if = { limit = { any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } } random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = relevant_movement } }` | 欢迎他们的支持。`add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_national_unions }` 利用他们为我们的全国劳工组织招募成员。`custom_tooltip = { text = corporatist_leaders_boost_tt scope:trade_unions_ig = { set_variable = { name = corporatist_leaders_boost days = very_long_modifier_time } } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_national_unions multiplier = 0.5 }` 这是打击反对派声望的绝佳机会。`trigger = { any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state }random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_opposition_discredited days = short_modifier_time } }` |
| 社会化 corporate_state.2 我国政府内部的一些人已开始辩论在拟议的 [current law] 框架下将关键产业国有化。许多私人企业家反对该提案。工厂主是个矮小的男人，靠厚底鞋、丰满的脸庞和一顶气派的帽子撑起了虚高的身量。这显然是他幸运地位的益处，也是他深有自觉的事实。"我从十三岁起就当鞋匠，"他开始说道，朝着窗外车间方向激动地比划。"我的师傅是个了不起的人。我二十岁上从他手里继承了店铺，九年后买下了这家工厂。没有我，这个镇子什么都不是。你明白吗？我不想听这些。我不想听。" | 社团主义国家 | 不可调和的分歧 corporate_state.3 [ relevant_state state name ]的 [ relevant_building building name ]中发生的残酷劳工虐待事件的消息已传遍全国。持不同政见者以此作为证据，指责政府拟议的 [current law] 无法保障工人的权利。"每一天，我们都听到关于我们的阶级兄弟在雇主的掠夺中失去生命——无论是指整个生命还是部分身体——的故事。面对这一指责，雇主们回答说他们同样背负重担，他们供养我们的程度不亚于我们供养他们。那么，为什么我们从未听说老板们失去手指、失去视力？为什么他们在六十五岁去世，而我们在三十岁就死去？我们的尸体被运出他们的工厂已成惯例，而他们可能遭受的最糟糕的事，不过是沦为像我们一样的人！" | 社团主义国家 社会君主制 | 拖延 | `OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } }` | `random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes any_scope_pop = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } } save_scope_as = relevant_state random_scope_pop = { limit = { strata = lower is_employed = yes NOR = { is_pop_type = peasants is_pop_type = slaves } standard_of_living < state.average_expected_sol } save_scope_as = relevant_pop workplace = { save_scope_as = relevant_building } } }` | 宣布对业主提起法律诉讼。`ig:ig_industrialists ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_legal_prosecution days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses }` 在此事进一步传播前将其压制。`add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses_suppressed }` 我们何必在乎一些 [ relevant_pop pop culture name ]的废物？`trigger = { scope:relevant_pop = { pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4 } }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals culture = scope:relevant_pop.culture }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_abuses multiplier = 0.5 }` |
| 剩余范畴 corporate_state.4 通过明显偏袒被视为具有生产性的就业部门，政府成功地为建立 [current law] 赢得了支持。反对者则被斥为不事生产和寄生成性。"当人们分析资本主义经济中的生产总量时，会得出这样的结论：人口可以分为两个主要范畴。生产性范畴——即那些对自然进行物质改造的人——和剩余范畴，即不从事此类活动的人。前者创造价值，后者贪婪地消耗价值。在资本主义社会中，后一范畴包括资本家本人、各类官僚、治安力量、职员、专业人士以及'服务工作者'。前一范畴通过创造自我再生产的条件来证明自身的存在——那么后者呢？" | 社团主义国家 社会君主制 | 推进 | `OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy }NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_multicultural }` | `random_primary_culture = { save_scope_as = primary_culture_scope }` | 高质量的工作是 [ primary_culture_scope culture name ]的传统。`add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_labor_chauvinism days = normal_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_productive_labor }` 为了生产阶级的诚实政权！`add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_class_unity days = normal_modifier_time }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_productive_labor }` |

| 无资格领导 corporate_state.6 建立 [current law] 的提案遭到 [ relevant_ig interestgroup name ]的抵制，他们声称该提案将允许不够格的工人对其企业拥有过多控制权。"在我的记忆中，我们一直遵守着同一份契约。我们为为我们工作的人提供指导，而指导的质量取决于我的裁量。我有幸在家具制造行业工作了四十年……你想让这些人来匹敌我的权威，他们中有多少人是识字的？多少人拥有学位？"桌后的官员一边听着业主说话一边点头，眉头紧锁。这个人感到了威胁，他提醒自己，而且他是一片好意。这话是真是假无关紧要——正是这句口头禅让他心安。"那么，我们不妨谈谈你现在的中层管理人员？" | 社团主义国家 社会君主制 | 拖延 | `OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state is_enacting_law = law_type:law_social_monarchy }any_interest_group = { OR = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie } ig_approval < 0 ig_counts_as_marginal = no }` | `random_interest_group = { limit = { OR = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie } ig_approval < 0 ig_counts_as_marginal = no } save_scope_as = relevant_ig }` | 向他们保证我们尊重私有财产权。`add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_private_property_rights }` 为这些企业发布豁免条款。`add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_private_property_carveouts }` 他们无权谈论资质问题。`add_enactment_setback = 1 scope:relevant_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_corporate_state_private_property_ignored_ig days = normal_modifier_time } }` |

| 军方支持 distribution_of_power_laws.4 出于对正在进行的转向 [current law] 所引发紧张局势的担忧， [ supportive_commander character name ]已表达对新法律的支持，并承诺抵制被剥夺选举权者可能发动的起义。"我并不是说我预期会发生叛乱，绝非如此。你们似乎已经完全掌控了局势。不过，在政权更迭之时，人们很容易忘记一场政治分歧能多快演变成军事问题。我和许多人一样，原则上支持这项改革，将其视为让这个经常自相矛盾的国家走向秩序与统一的一步。更重要的是，我在实践中支持它。相信我，出自一个军 [man/woman]之口，这才是真正有分量的支持。" | 独裁制 长者议会 土地投票制 寡头制 财富投票制 | 推进 | `OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_autocracy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_oligarchy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_elder_council is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_census_voting has_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage }any_scope_character = { OR = { has_role_of_type = general has_role_of_type = admiral } OR = { law_stance = { #they approve law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } interest_group ?= { #their ig approves law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } } }NOT = { has_modifier = fear_of_popular_uprising }NOT = { has_modifier = senior_military_support }` | `random_scope_character = { limit = { OR = { has_role_of_type = general has_role_of_type = admiral } OR = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } interest_group ?= { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } } } save_scope_as = supportive_commander }` | 面对无法无天的民众，我们需要 [his/her]的支持！`add_enactment_modifier = { name = fear_of_popular_uprising }` [He/she]是我方阵营中忠诚的绝佳典范！`add_enactment_modifier = { name = senior_military_support }add_loyalists = { pop_type = officers value = 0.3 }` |

| 旧权威 distribution_of_power_laws.9 [current law]改革威胁到了[country]许多权势人物手中的权力，其中一些人正利用他们的政治人脉阻挠该法案通过。 “咱们私下说说，这件事里有太多好高骛远的理想主义者了。他们在放弃自己的影响力中找到某种高尚感，把它像施舍穷人的救济品一样散给大众。也许他们觉得这能拯救他们的灵魂，或者他们的身后名。而我更务实一些。我关心的是我和朋友们眼下的前途。你也算其中一员，对吧？” | 有限选举权 普选制 | 停滞 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage }NOR = { has_law = law_type:law_census_voting has_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage has_law = law_type:law_anarchy }NOT = { has_modifier = fresh_start }NOT = { has_modifier = collaboration_with_old_leadership } |  | 如果他们试图阻止新法律，就必须把他们赶下台！ trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy } add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = capitalists } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_landed_voting } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = aristocrats } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = officers } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = clergymen } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = capitalists } }if = { limit = { has_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting } add_radicals = { value = small_radicals strata = upper } } 也许我们可以在建立新制度的过程中与他们合作。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = collaboration_with_old_leadership } |
| 过往之罪 distribution_of_power_laws.10 [current law]改革的支持者们提请人们关注现政权过去滥用权力的行为，以此说明这项改革为何刻不容缓。 “说实话，我自己都几乎不记得了。那是另一个时代。这种事情就是会发生，我也从没想过会因此被质询。倒也不是说当时没人质疑，但抱怨的人总是有的。要是想做成事，就不可能去听他们的。当然，这一切都是得到[title]默许的。不是官方层面的，但我认为你应该知道。这话就咱们之间说说。” | 无政府状态 有限选举权 土地投票制 普选制 财富投票制 | 推进 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anarchy }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_single_party_state }NOT = { has_modifier = admitted_abuses_of_power }NOT = { has_modifier = downplayed_abuses_of_power } |  | 权力导致腐败，因此掌权者必须接受选民的问责。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = admitted_abuses_of_power }every_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes } leader = { add_modifier = { name = corrupted_by_power days = normal_modifier_time } } } 过去的就让它过去吧。这些问题与改革毫无关系。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = downplayed_abuses_of_power } |
| 没有人民的民主 distribution_of_power_laws.11 普选制的支持者认为，正在进行的[current law]改革是一个不充分的折中方案，将许多人排斥在参与民主的权利之外。 “‘[Current law]’。听听这名字！仿佛这样我们就能挣脱压迫的枷锁，而不仅仅是换个肩膀来扛。当这项改革仍让那么多人无法获得选举权时，我们怎么可能团结一致地支持它？和往常一样，他们被指望为‘总算有点变化’而感恩戴德，对自己的生活则不抱任何期待。这不是对待一个人的方式。” | 有限选举权 土地投票制 财富投票制 | 辩论 | has_technology_researched = egalitarianism OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_landed_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting }OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy has_law = law_type:law_single_party_state }any_political_movement = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } NOT = { has_modifier = fear_of_future_reforms } }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage value > neutral } ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_modifier = current_election_focus } | law_type:law_universal_suffrage = { save_scope_as = universal_suffrage_type } | 我们应专注于当前的改革，这已是明显的进步。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = current_election_focus }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage value > neutral } } add_modifier = { name = democratic_compromise days = normal_modifier_time } } [Current law]是通向普选制道路上的一块踏脚石！ random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = fear_of_future_reforms days = normal_modifier_time } } |

| 增长超出预期 economy_laws.1 中央经济部门宣布，我们将经济事务纳入政府管控的努力带来了令人惊喜的积极增长。 那人紧张地扶了扶眼镜。 “说实话，我自己都惊呆了，”他承认道，“我把数字算了两遍，还让办公室里三个职员核对我的工作，结果你猜怎么着？我们进度超前，增长率也超出了预期！哎呀，我看我得庆祝一下了！”他伸手去解领结，摸索了一会儿之后把它摘了下来。“都留神点，今晚我要痛快放纵一场！”他兴高采烈地喊道。 | 计划经济 | 推进 |  |  | 太好了！继续推进立法。 trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 太好了！让我们支持促成这一切的职员们。 add_modifier = { name = projected_growth_models_bureau days = short_modifier_time } |

| 无可辩驳的敲诈 economy_laws.4 一位来自[ pro_free_trade_ig interestgroup name ]的演说家在全国各地巡回演讲，谴责[standing law]，颂扬自由贸易的价值。 “当我们考虑到我们伟大体制的理论保证每个人充分享有其勤劳与进取的全部成果时，很明显，任何阻碍商品自由流通的行为都是无可辩驳的敲诈，是对我们价值观的可耻背叛。” | 自由贸易 | 推进 | any_interest_group = { is_in_government = yes law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_law = { limit = { type = law_type:law_mercantilism # To get the trade law category } currently_active_law_in_group = { save_scope_as = standing_law_scope } } random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = pro_free_trade_ig } | 我们正朝着一个共同目标努力！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = freeing_the_markets } [ pro_free_trade_ig interestgroup name ]中真有一位了不起的演说家。 scope:pro_free_trade_ig = { add_modifier = { name = free_market_ideologue days = short_modifier_time } } |
| 思想的自由市场 economy_laws.5 我们计划中的经济改革的反对者要求获得与我们同等的言论表达机会，令人恼火的是，他们竟然用我们自己的自由市场说辞来为自己的言论自由辩护。 “我们想要的只是……思想的自由贸易。” | 自由贸易 | 辩论 | not = { has_variable = free_trade_law_debate_timer }any_interest_group = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = opposing_free_trade_ig } set_variable = { name = free_trade_law_debate_timer days = 730 # two years } | 我们要的是自由市场，不是空谈！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_debate }scope:opposing_free_trade_ig = { add_modifier = { name = no_free_exchange days = short_modifier_time } } 竞争是必要的，思想领域也不例外。 random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_debate_success } } 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_debate_fail } } } 在自由的交锋中，真理必胜！ scope:opposing_free_trade_ig = { add_modifier = { name = free_exchange_of_ideas days = short_modifier_time } } |
| [ industrial_protectionist_state state name ]的工业受损 economy_laws.6 来自国外的竞争商品导致[ industrial_protectionist_state state name ]的产品需求下滑，引发了该州对我们计划改革的担忧。 “自由贸易把我们的金钱和市场拱手让人，损害了我们的劳动、我们的工业和我们的人民。如果这些政策不能造福我们，那它们还有什么用？” | 自由贸易 | 停滞 | any_scope_state = { OR = { has_industry_building = yes has_building = building_shipyard has_building = building_arms_industry has_heavy_industry_building = yes } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { OR = { has_industry_building = yes has_building = building_shipyard has_building = building_arms_industry has_heavy_industry_building = yes } } save_scope_as = industrial_protectionist_state } | 我们必须缓解[ industrial_protectionist_state state name ]的担忧。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_stall } 我们必须努力让[ industrial_protectionist_state state name ]相信我们改革的优点。 trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_stall_success multiplier = 0.667 } 无论如何我们都必须继续推进我们的计划。 scope:industrial_protectionist_state = { add_modifier = { name = industrial_concerns_ignored days = short_modifier_time } }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_stall_fail } } 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_trade_stall_success } } } |
| 学术协助 education_laws.1 我们高等学府的教师们主动提出，就新的[current law]的实施向政府提供建议。“教孩子们基础知识和我们大学所提供的更高深的教育之间当然存在差异。然而，作为这座巍峨知识大厦上层的建造者，我们认为自己至少可以为地基该如何搭建提供一些见解。” | Private Schools Public Schools Religious Schools | Advance | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }any_scope_state = { has_building = building_university }NOT = { has_modifier = assisting_professors }NOT = { has_modifier = university_support } |  | 我们很乐意有他们的帮助！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = assisting_professors } 他们的研究工作更重要。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = university_support } 他们或许能帮助敲定法案。 trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 |
| 规训与惩罚 education_laws.2 武装力量提出了纪律在新[current law]中的作用，认为这是新教育体系规划中需要改进的方面。“这不仅仅是为了维持井然有序的课堂以便传授知识，更是为了塑造品格。毕竟，这些孩子的父母竭尽全力将他们培养成正直的公民，把如此重大的责任托付给了我们。我们经常发现，前来服役的男孩们早已因放荡不羁的少年时代和对权威的漠视而性格尽毁，几乎无可挽回。事情本不必如此！” | Private Schools Public Schools Religious Schools | Debate | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }NOT = { has_modifier = curriculum_focus }NOT = { has_modifier = discipline_focus }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_prussian_education } } | ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { save_scope_as = armed_forces_ig } | 我们应该专注于课程本身。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = curriculum_focus }ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = undisciplined_children days = normal_modifier_time } } 将其融入学校体系至关重要。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = discipline_focus }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_prussian_education sponsor = scope:armed_forces_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| 课程分歧 education_laws.3 政府内部关于新[current law]应教授何种内容的分歧使改革陷入停滞。“这些课程或许在两个世纪前很适合用来教导贵族子弟，但认为它们在当今时代也能很好地服务于更广泛的学生，简直是彻头彻尾的疯狂！”“照你的意思是说，你的那些‘现代’替代方案就能更好地服务他们？你想让古典学科被逐步淘汰，换来的却是无知和拙劣的语法？” | Private Schools Public Schools Religious Schools | Stall | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_religious_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_schools is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_schools }NOT = { has_modifier = curriculum_compromise }NOT = { has_modifier = extended_curriculum_discussions } |  | 强行达成妥协。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = curriculum_compromise multiplier = 0.5 }every_interest_group = { limit = { is_in_government = yes } add_modifier = { name = compromised_on_schools days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = 2 } } 花些时间化解分歧。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = extended_curriculum_discussions } 重新调整法律，为此设立一个独立机构。 trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 |
| 编外教师 education_laws.4 有在家教育孩子经验的家长们主动提出在乡村地区担任教师，以推动新[current law]体系的落实。“问问我的任何一位邻居，他们都会为我对社区做的事作证，尽管我没有正式资质！这一带凡是已经识字或会写字的孩子，都是我教的。对我来说，这比我亲手做出的任何东西都更值得骄傲。” | Public Schools | Advance | NOT = { has_modifier = local_teachers } | set_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod value = 1 } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.10 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.20 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.30 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.40 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.50 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.60 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.70 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.80 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } if = { limit = { literacy_rate >= 0.90 } change_variable = { name = literacy_teacher_mod add = 1 } } | 我们已有受过教育的人，真是幸运！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = local_teachers multiplier = var:literacy_teacher_mod }remove_variable = literacy_teacher_mod custom_tooltip = education_laws.4.tt 我们不能因此耽误其他工作。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = local_school_support multiplier = var:literacy_teacher_mod }remove_variable = literacy_teacher_mod custom_tooltip = education_laws.4.tt |
| 男孩与女孩 education_laws.5 关于新[current law]是否应为男女学生分设课堂、甚至采用不同课程的争论，正在新教育体系的实施上引发分歧。“会有人质疑两性天性上的差异，或他们在我们社会中由天命注定的角色吗？如果我们在教育上不做这种区分，又怎能指望培养出真正的淑女与绅士？” | Public Schools | Debate | NOT = { has_modifier = coeducational_schools }NOT = { has_modifier = single_sex_education }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace value > neutral } }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_no_womens_rights value > neutral } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { OR = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_co_ed_schools has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_single_sex_education } } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace value > neutral } } save_scope_as = pro_women_ig } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_no_womens_rights value > neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_women_ig } | 这种观念是旧时代的残余。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = coeducational_schools }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_no_womens_rights value > neutral } } add_modifier = { name = mixed_sex_education days = normal_modifier_time } }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_co_ed_schools sponsor = scope:pro_women_ig cooldown = 120 } } 我们应该设立两个独立的分支。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = single_sex_education }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_single_sex_education sponsor = scope:anti_women_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| 学校组织 education_laws.6 在[country]，虔信派历来承担着部分教育职责。随着新[current law]体系的建立，他们在组织这些学校方面的角色问题成为改革的焦点。“当然，国家仍将是学校事务优先级的最高权威，但地方层面还有许多行政事务需要打理。况且，[religion]经典本就已是学习计划的一部分，不是吗？” | Public Schools | Stall | NOT = { has_modifier = church_school_organization }NOT = { has_modifier = building_secular_school_organization }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { OR = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_church_organised_schools has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_secular_school_system } } } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig } | 组织职责应交给虔信派。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = church_school_organization }ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = public_school_responsibility days = normal_modifier_time } }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_church_organised_schools sponsor = scope:devout_ig cooldown = 120 } } 我们需要从头建立一套世俗学校体系。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = building_secular_school_organization }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_secular_school_system sponsor = scope:intelligentsia_ig cooldown = 120 } } |

| 过度狂热的审查 free_speech_laws.3 [current law]法律难以获得民众支持，因为相关言论正遭到现行审查机构的压制。“凡是涉及审查的内容都会立刻被审查，那我们还怎么就这个问题展开公开讨论？”“头一回见吧？他们只懂他们理解的东西。如果他们没多少文化，你完全可以借几个比喻和明喻蒙混过关。”“明喻到底是什么鬼？某种扭曲的微笑吗？” | Protected Speech Right of Assembly | Stall | NOR = { has_modifier = free_speech_censorship_stall has_modifier = free_speech_censorship_stall_fail has_modifier = free_speech_censorship_stall_success }AND = { OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_right_of_assembly is_enacting_law = law_type:law_protected_speech } OR = { has_law = law_type:law_censorship has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = book_burning_ig } | 我们无能为力。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_speech_censorship_stall } 也许我们可以在法律边缘打点擦边球。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_speech_censorship_stall_success }scope:book_burning_ig ?= { add_modifier = { name = free_speech_censorship_stall_success_ig days = short_modifier_time } } |

| 受伤的农民 healthcare_laws.1 [ farming_accidents_state state name ]的农民们一直在向政府申诉他们所用设备的危险性，并希望有某种医疗保健来减轻他们的困苦。“前几天邻居来我家。他站在我门口，左手紧紧攥着右手，我这才意识到他切掉了三根手指。我不是医生，但他需要帮助，我尽力做了我能做的。只希望当时能有个更懂行的人可以让他去找。” | Charity Hospitals Private Health Insurance Public Health Insurance | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_no_health_system or = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_health_insurance is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance }NOT = { has_modifier = farming_accident_chance }any_scope_state = { any_scope_building = { OR = { is_building_type = building_rye_farm is_building_type = building_wheat_farm is_building_type = building_rice_farm is_building_type = building_maize_farm is_building_type = building_millet_farm } NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_tools_disabled } } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { any_scope_building = { OR = { is_building_type = building_rye_farm is_building_type = building_wheat_farm is_building_type = building_rice_farm is_building_type = building_maize_farm is_building_type = building_millet_farm } NOT = { has_active_production_method = pm_tools_disabled } } } save_scope_as = farming_accidents_state } | 尽力提供帮助。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = farming_accident_chance } 专注于建立医疗保健体系。 scope:farming_accidents_state = { add_modifier = { name = farming_accidents_mort days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = farming_accident_chance multiplier = 4 } 反正这也不是给穷人用的。 trigger = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_health_insurance }scope:farming_accidents_state = { add_modifier = { name = farming_accidents_mort days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = 3 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = farming_accident_chance multiplier = 5 } |
| 医疗税 healthcare_laws.2 随着建立某种医疗保健体系的呼声，许多人开始质疑建立新医疗保健体系的资金从何而来。有人建议直接从税收资金中拨出，也有人坚持政府应另寻他法来承担费用。“让我搞清楚，你指望我们不但为自己的医疗付费，还要为别人的买单？我不希望我缴纳的税款被用来让随便哪个农民能看得起病。那不是我的责任。我还要考虑自己的健康和家庭。我不可能还负担得起照顾[country]的其他所有人。” | Charity Hospitals Private Health Insurance Public Health Insurance | Debate | has_law = law_type:law_no_health_system or = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system is_enacting_law = law_type:law_private_health_insurance is_enacting_law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance }NOT = { has_modifier = taxing_health } |  | 从税收资金中拨出。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = health_tax multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small }add_enactment_modifier = { name = industrialist_tax }add_enactment_modifier = { name = taxing_health multiplier = 2 } 政府应承担这笔费用。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = government_health_tax multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small }add_enactment_modifier = { name = taxing_health multiplier = 3 } 没有它我们也能撑过去。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = taxing_health } |

| 医疗不足 healthcare_laws.4 由于公共医疗服务短缺，全国各地负担不起自身医疗费用的公民开始要求更易获得、更充分的医疗保健。“你花一整天收割过庄稼或开采过石头吗？我想没有。那是艰苦折磨人的活儿，偶尔还会出事故。我们希望得到保证：一旦受伤，能够得到照料。我们还要养家糊口。如果我们受了重伤无法康复，又怎么照顾他们？” | Public Health Insurance | Advance | or = { has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system has_law = law_type:law_private_health_insurance }NOT = { has_modifier = more_healthcare } |  | 腾出一些额外的行政力。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = more_healthcare }add_enactment_modifier = { name = health_bureaucracy } 专注于通过法律。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = more_healthcare multiplier = 2 } |
| 可得性与可及性 healthcare_laws.5 [ anti_public_healthcare_advocate character name ]（[ anti_public_healthcare_ig interestgroup name ]的领袖）在今天的辩论中提出了担忧：医疗保健的普及可能使其更容易获得，但对原本已享有医疗的人来说却变得供不应求。“你觉得会发生什么？如果我们向公众开放医疗可及性，你总不能指望可得性不受影响吧。人们要等上几天、几周甚至几个月才能看上一次医生。到时候我们该如何在不同的病情与人们的等候时间之间权衡取舍。” | Public Health Insurance | Debate | or = { has_law = law_type:law_charitable_health_system has_law = law_type:law_private_health_insurance }NOT = { has_modifier = opening_up_healthcare }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance value < neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_public_health_insurance value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_public_healthcare_ig leader = { save_scope_as = anti_public_healthcare_advocate } } | 他们的担忧毫无根据。 ai_chance = { base = 50 modifier = { trigger = { scope:anti_public_healthcare_ig = { ig_approval < -5 } } add = -25 } }scope:anti_public_healthcare_ig = { add_modifier = { name = healthcare_debate_ig_ignored days = normal_modifier_time } }random_list = { 9 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = opening_up_healthcare multiplier = 2 } } 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = opening_up_healthcare multiplier = 4 } } } 他们的担忧应当得到考虑。 ai_chance = { base = 50 }scope:anti_public_healthcare_ig = { add_modifier = { name = healthcare_debate_ig_concerns_heard days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = opening_up_healthcare multiplier = -1 } |

| 解放者 labor_rights.2 由[ anti_serfdom_advocate character name ]领导的一群有影响力的[ anti_serfdom_ig interestgroup name ]正在请愿，要求迅速废除农奴制。“我可以整日滔滔不绝地讲述解放的道德美德，而且理由充分。但问题的简单真相在于：农奴制是低效的。它在经济上是灾难性的，是我们国家在工业与社会上正当发展的阻碍。在田间劳作的农奴若被派往工厂，能获得有报酬的雇佣并真正勤奋地工作，会更有价值。” | 集体化农业 商业化农业 自耕农土地制 农民所有制 佃农制 | 辩论 | has_law = law_type:law_serfdom OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_tenant_farmers is_enacting_law = law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture is_enacting_law = law_type:law_homesteading is_enacting_law = law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship is_enacting_law = law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_serfdom value < neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = anti_serfdom_debate } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_serfdom value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_serfdom_ig leader = { save_scope_as = anti_serfdom_advocate } } | 他们的论据十分有力。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = 1 } 多么鼓舞人心的演说家！ scope:anti_serfdom_advocate = { add_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_popularity days = normal_modifier_time } }random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = 3 } } 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = anti_serfdom_debate multiplier = -1 } } } |

| 先发制人 labour_associations_events.2 政府将[current law]写入法律的消息在工人阶层社区中迅速传开。实业家开始抱怨非法工会活动随之抬头。政府一宣布这项举措，海报便开始出现。守夜人抓捕肇事者的徒劳努力一无所获，迫使公司进行危机公关。每天夜里，墙上都被贴满——到了早晨，公司用前一晚的成果装满了垃圾桶。就在下一周，清洁工们宣布成立了他们的新工会。 | Right to Associate | Advance | OR = { AND = { c:BIC ?= this ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } AND = { NOT = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } } | if = { limit = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } | 命令法院无视这些投诉。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_preempetive_strikes }scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_preempetive_strikes_ig days = short_modifier_time } } 现行法律继续有效。 scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_slow_and_boring days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = machinists }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = laborers } |
| 工会委员会 labour_associations_events.3 在劳工团体的请愿之后，[legislature]组建了一个关于[current law]的委员会。委员会向政府提交了两份报告——一份由[ opposed_ig interestgroup name ]撰写的多数派报告，以及一份同情[ unions_ig interestgroup name ]的少数派报告。 “事实上，现行法律是片面而不公的。因为几乎任何工人为实现自身目标而结成的联合都被视为限制贸易的阴谋，从而应受刑事处罚，而雇主犯下完全相似的行为却逃脱了法律的注意，理由是不构成共谋罪。但事实上，雇佣数千工人的雇主本身就是一个联合体。” | Labor association | Debate | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes country_has_voting_franchise = yes any_interest_group = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_ig } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = opposed_ig } | 采纳少数派报告。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_favorable_trade_union_report }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_favorable_report days = short_modifier_time } }scope:opposed_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_favorable_report_cons days = short_modifier_time } } 多数派报告更有说服力。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_hostile_trade_union_report }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_hostile_report_tus days = short_modifier_time } }scope:opposed_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_accepted_hostile_report days = short_modifier_time } } |
| 垂死的行业 labour_associations_events.4 政府对行会制度的打击在那些感到生计受到威胁的人中引发了骚乱。在[ relevant_state state name]，闹事的工匠和熟练工人砸毁机器，恐吓承包商。 诅咒那我们在饥饿之际 徒然向他祈求的上帝； 我们白白地盼望，白白地等待， 他只嘲弄讥笑我们悲惨的命运，—— 我们在织，我们在织！ | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions Right to Associate | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_guild_system OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes turmoil >= 0.1 any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = shopkeepers } any_scope_building = { is_building_group = bg_manufacturing } } | ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = pb_ig } random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes turmoil >= 0.1 any_scope_pop = { is_pop_type = shopkeepers } any_scope_building = { is_building_group = bg_manufacturing } } save_scope_as = relevant_state } | 如果他们无法自由竞争，大可以去别处找工作。 scope:relevant_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = large_radicals } state_region = { add_devastation = 10 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_artisans_uprising } 派陆军去保护财产。 scope:relevant_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = large_radicals } state_region = { add_devastation = 5 } }scope:pb_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_repressed_artisans_riots days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_artisans_uprising multiplier = 0.35 } |
| 最后一搏 labour_associations_events.5 政府颁布[current law]的努力遭到工会近乎普遍的反对。工会领导层宣布举行总罢工，试图迫使政府让步。 工会倾巢而出。[legislature]一英里内的每一根灯柱都挂满了红色飘带，步行通行已成奢望。几名政府立法者选择睡在办公室里，以免艰难穿越人潮。其他人，即反对派中的激进分子，则选择加入其中。他们发现回去的路已被一路蜿蜒环绕整栋建筑的警察防线切断。双方现在都将其视为围困状态。抗议者建立了一套真正令人叹服的后勤系统在内部分配面包和水，而立法者们则征用了[legislature]的食堂和酒窖。 “还要多久？”一名资浅议员问道。他的同僚已无力再回答。 | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions | Stall | has_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions }ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = trade_unions_ig } | 他们的反应正好证明该法案合理。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_strikes_stall multiplier = 0.5 }add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time }add_radicals = { pop_type = machinists value = medium_radicals }add_radicals = { pop_type = laborers value = medium_radicals } 提出谈判。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_strikes_stall }add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 }add_radicals = { pop_type = machinists value = small_radicals }add_radicals = { pop_type = laborers value = small_radicals } 截断他们的通信手段。 trigger = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_censorship has_law = law_type:law_outlawed_dissent } }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_modifier = { name = modifier_nationwide_strikes days = short_modifier_time multiplier = 0.5 } |
| 难以忍受的纷争 labour_associations_events.6 一本详述产业工人日常所受骇人境遇的书一夜之间大获成功。这本书的成功提升了公众对[current law]的关注，同时也让实业家难堪。 “于是这种社会秩序使工人的家庭生活几乎无法维持。在一处凄凉肮脏、仅够夜宿的屋子里，家具残缺，往往既不避雨也不保暖，污浊的空气充斥着拥挤不堪的房间，任何家庭的温馨都无从谈起。丈夫整日工作，妻子或许还有年长的孩子也各自在不同地点劳作；他们只在夜晚和清晨相见，人人都时刻受到酗酒的诱惑；在这样的条件下，还能有什么家庭生活？” |  | Debate | enacting_more_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes OR = { AND = { c:BIC ?= this ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } AND = { NOT = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } } | if = { limit = { c:BIC ?= this } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = industrialists_ig } } | 工人应当能够为自己发声。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_conditions_of_the_working_class }scope:industrialists_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_conditions_of_the_working_class_ig days = short_modifier_time } } 我们决不能再触怒雇主们。 add_loyalists = { pop_type = capitalists value = medium_radicals }add_loyalists = { pop_type = shopkeepers value = small_radicals } |
| 法律条文 labour_associations_events.7 受政府转向打压有组织劳工的鼓舞，[ relevant_bourgeois_ig interestgroup name ]开始在其店铺内抢先镇压工会。在旧法律仍然有效之际，[ unions_ig interestgroup name ]要求政府作出回应。 这实在荒谬。书记员清楚，他的上司清楚，站在他面前的工会代表恐怕也清楚。一份最后一刻递交的请愿书，援引的是一部在其到达法官手中之前就会从法典上消失的法律。然而，它又是完美的。它没有任何明显的错误能让他当场驳回。他唯一的选择就是让它去堵住别人的收件箱。他的嘴角挤出一个毫无喜悦的微笑。 “谢谢您，先生。部里会以邮件方式与您联系。” |  | Debate | enacting_less_permissive_labour_associations_law = yes OR = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no } } | ig:ig_trade_unions ?= { save_scope_as = unions_ig } if = { limit = { OR = { ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie.ig_clout > ig:ig_industrialists.ig_clout c:BIC ?= this } } ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie ?= { save_scope_as = relevant_bourgeois_ig } } else = { ig:ig_industrialists ?= { save_scope_as = relevant_bourgeois_ig } } | 他们已不再具备任何法律地位。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_tolerated_union_crackdown }scope:relevant_bourgeois_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_tolerated_union_crackdown_ig days = short_modifier_time } }add_radicals = { value = small_radicals pop_type = laborers }add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals pop_type = machinists } 我们必须维护现行立法。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_punished_union_crackdown }scope:relevant_bourgeois_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_punished_union_crackdown_ig days = short_modifier_time } } |
| 劳动的自由市场 labour_associations_events.8 随着政府着手废除行会制度，受到鼓舞的劳工和机械师开始进入他们从前被排斥的行业。 “我要走了。本想早点说的。我在[ relevant_state state cityhubname]找了份工作，在工厂里。他们给我提供了路费贷款，还有一张铺位……”他话音渐止，依次望向朋友们的脸。“谢谢你们。谢谢这一切。我会写信的，或者请人代笔。这段日子……这段日子很美好。总有一天我们会再见的。我保证。” | Anti-Strike Laws Combination Acts Corporatized Unions Right to Associate | Advance | has_law = law_type:law_guild_system OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_combination_acts is_enacting_law = law_type:law_anti_strike_laws is_enacting_law = law_type:law_right_to_associate is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporatized_unions } | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } save_scope_as = relevant_state } | 我们的努力得到了丰厚回报。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_guilds_abolition_benefit } 这是自由的伟大胜利。 add_modifier = { name = modifier_guilds_abolished_mobility days = normal_modifier_time } |

| 世俗教义 law_events.6 知识分子们对 [current law] 法律的通过表示不满，认为教育与宗教应当分离。“如果孩子们从一开始就被灌输思想，我们怎么能指望他们学到任何有意义的东西？” | 宗教学校 | Country_event | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { ig_counts_as_marginal = no }NOT = { has_variable = intelligentsia_disapprove_religious_schools} | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { save_scope_as = intelligentsia_ig } set_variable = { name = intelligentsia_disapprove_religious_schools days = normal_modifier_time } | 我们会加以考虑。 add_enactment_modifier = { #law takes more time to pass name = religious_schools_good_modifier }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_religious_schools_abolished_intelligentsia days = normal_modifier_time } } 免谈。 add_enactment_modifier = { #you get some success thing name = religious_schools_bad_modifier }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { add_modifier = { name = modifier_ignored_intelligentsia_request days = normal_modifier_time } } |

| 反对派成员提出要求 generic_laws.3 目前反对通过 [current law] 的 [ opposition_demands_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 中较为务实的成员表示，如果获得某些政治让步，他们愿意改变立场。然而，这样的让步不会受到 [ opposition_demands_supporting_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 的欢迎。“妥协是把好伞，却是糟糕的屋顶；它是权宜之计，在政党政治中往往明智，在治国之道上几乎必然失策。” |  | 辩论 | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = opposition_demands_ig_scope } random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = opposition_demands_supporting_ig_scope } | 答应他们的要求，法案必须通过。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = cross_bench_support }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase sponsor = scope:opposition_demands_ig_scope cooldown = 60 } }scope:opposition_demands_supporting_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = unpopular_compromises days = normal_modifier_time } } 妥协是弱者的工具。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = political_advances_spurned }scope:opposition_demands_supporting_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = steadfast_conviction days = normal_modifier_time } } 这是打破法案僵局的绝佳机会。 trigger = { enactment_phase < 2 }add_enactment_phase = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_short_term_benefit }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_generic_sponsor_polstr_increase sponsor = scope:opposition_demands_ig_scope cooldown = 60 } }scope:opposition_demands_supporting_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = unpopular_compromises days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = 2 } } |

| 官僚泥潭 generic_laws.5 致力于通过 [current law] 的立法者们发现自己深陷一套格外繁复的官僚程序之中，改革工作几乎陷于停顿。充分发展的官僚机构与其他组织相比，恰如机器与非机械生产方式之比。也就是说，其各个齿轮的齿必须相互啮合，否则齿轮便会嘎吱作响、剧烈翻搅，直至最终断裂。 |  | 停滞 | enacting_any_law = yes | random_scope_state = { limit = { is_capital = yes } save_scope_as = imbroglio_state_scope } | 这该死的迷宫般的法律体系！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = mired_in_bureaucracy } 至少砍掉一些繁文缛节。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = mired_in_bureaucracy_minor }add_modifier = { name = cutting_red_tape days = normal_modifier_time }scope:imbroglio_state_scope = { add_radicals_in_state = { # bureaucracts pissed off value = large_radicals pop_type = bureaucrats } } |
| [Title] 干预政治进程 generic_laws.6 [title] 亲自过问有关 [current law] 的持续辩论，并决意行使 [his/her] 王室特权以确保该法律获得通过。[Ruler] 极少直接干预法律事务，而是依靠委托体系与个人影响力来高效治理 [his/her] 的国度。虽然在法律上 [title] 可以随意行使 [his/her] 王室权力，但这样做必定会激怒共和主义者以及该法律的反对者。 | Corporate State Council Republic Parliamentary Republic Presidential Republic Theocracy | 辩论 | has_law = law_type:law_monarchy enacting_any_law = yes exists = currently_enacting_law.type NOR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_council_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_theocracy is_enacting_law = law_type:law_corporate_state }ruler = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } |  | 只需要 [title] 的批准即可。 trigger = { OR = { has_law = law_type:law_autocracy has_law = law_type:law_oligarchy } }add_enactment_phase = 1 every_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no OR = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader } law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } NOT = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } } add_modifier = { name = royal_prerogative days = normal_modifier_time multiplier = 1.5 } } [title] 的意愿必将成为现实！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = backed_by_court }every_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no OR = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_republican_leader } law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } NOT = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } } add_modifier = { name = royal_prerogative days = normal_modifier_time } } 劝阻 [title] 不要这么做，这会弊大于利。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = ruler_restrained } |
| 法律的民间支持 generic_laws.7 下层阶级的政治意愿与诉求永远是个难解之谜。不知何故，对 [current law] 的支持已如野火般在 [country] 蔓延开来，魅力四射的演说者与小型社交俱乐部中热烈的讨论推波助澜。“他们在支持什么？好吧，我们又有什么好抱怨的。虽然他们的支持从来不是成功的必要条件，但有总比没有好。” |  | 推进 | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } }any_scope_state = { is_incorporated = yes }any_political_movement = { law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = grassroots_ig_scope } random_scope_state = { limit = { is_incorporated = yes } save_scope_as = grassroots_state_scope } random_political_movement = { limit = { law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = supporting_movement } | 公众的热情加速改革！ if = { limit = { exists = scope:supporting_movement } scope:supporting_movement = { add_modifier = { name = grassroots_support_for_movement } } }else = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = wave_of_popular_support } }scope:grassroots_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = grassroots_support days = normal_modifier_time } } 借这一共同立场安抚民众。 scope:grassroots_state_scope = { add_loyalists_in_state = { strata = lower value = 0.2 } } |

| 法律提案在 [ non_accepted_culture_scope culture name ] 社群引发骚动 generic_laws.9 声称 [current law] 的通过将以格外负面的方式影响该国的 [ non_accepted_culture_scope culture name ] 居民，[ non_accepted_culture_scope culture name ] 的煽动者们在社群中煽动对当前法律提案的反对。“给他们一根手指，他们就会索要一条手臂。给他们一条手臂——不知不觉间——他们就会把手臂变成武器，用它把你所知的一切砸个稀烂，再从尸体上搜刮更多让步！” |  | 辩论 | law_can_hurt_minorities = yes # | [ defectors_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 内部的分歧 generic_laws.10 [ defectors_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 内部一个有影响力的派系对该集团其他成员在 [current law] 议题上的中立态度日益不满。他们自己支持该法律，如今决意另立一个志在通过该法律的政治派系。“你们怎能袖手旁观，眼看世界从眼前掠过？你们难道没有荣誉感吗？——没有对同胞的责任感吗？我对这种胸无大志说不得，而且我敢说我绝非唯一这么想的人。” |  | 推进 | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value = neutral } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value = neutral } } save_scope_as = defectors_ig_scope } random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_enactment_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = defectors_support_ig_scope } | 有了他们的支持，法案必将通过。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = pro_law_factionalism }scope:defectors_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = member_flight days = normal_modifier_time } } [ defectors_support_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 向这些特立独行者伸出橄榄枝。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = pro_law_factionalism_minor }scope:defectors_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = member_exodus days = normal_modifier_time } }scope:defectors_support_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = member_influx days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| 异见者倒戈 generic_laws.11 在外界观察者看来，[ dissenters_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 也许看似坚定支持 [current law]，但在这团结的表象之下，分歧早已暗生。如今，一个异见者小集团终于忍无可忍，正式因改革问题与同僚决裂。“我们并肩站立了多久？多少次我们向你们伸出援手？然而我们自己的关切无人回应，我们的诉求无人理会。如果在这里无人倾听我们，我们自会去别处发声。” |  | 停滞 | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = dissenters_ig_scope } | 分裂分子！让魔鬼把他们带走吧。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = political_discord }scope:dissenters_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = party_fragmentation days = normal_modifier_time } } 松一松钱袋，也许能把一些人拉回来。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = political_discord_minor }scope:dissenters_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = party_fragmentation_minor days = normal_modifier_time } }add_modifier = { name = greasing_palms multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small days = short_modifier_time } 让他们负责一份新草案。 trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_half_written_bill }scope:dissenters_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = party_fragmentation_minor days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| 不正当的关系 generic_laws.12 对围绕 [current law] 的漫长立法程序感到沮丧的 [ underhanded_instigator_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 成员 [ underhanded_instigator_leader_scope character name ] 找到我们，提出利用 [his/her] 在国家机构中的私人关系来加快进程。“我只求在合适的人耳边说上几句话。当然，一切都将在法律允许的范围之内，不过我可不能保证我们在此事上的对手会欣然看待这类行为。” |  | 辩论 | enacting_any_law = yes any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | every_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = underhanded_ig_scope } random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = underhanded_instigator_ig_scope leader = { save_scope_as = underhanded_instigator_leader_scope } } | 准许 [him/her]，时间紧迫。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = underhanded_tactics }scope:underhanded_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = dishonest_methods days = normal_modifier_time } } 不，我们要么照章办事，要么干脆不做。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = by_the_book } |
| 世界公民 migration_laws.1 受当前 [current law] 改革的启发，[ no_borders_visionary character interestgroup name ] 的 [ no_borders_visionary character name ] 撰写了一篇构想，描绘了一个彻底废除边界的未来。“边界是地图上的伤疤。如同伤疤，确实有人在某种意义上觉得它们美丽。来之不易，历经艰辛才赢得。它们美得让人忘记了地图本不需要伤疤，于是人们开始制造伤疤。在地图上，但更可怕的是，在人身上。当我们能够放下过去、让伤疤愈合的那一天，才是我们终获自由的那一天。” | Migration Controls No Migration Controls | 推进 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { NOT = { law_stance = { law = root.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } }NOT = { has_modifier = future_without_borders }NOT = { has_modifier = future_freer_movement } | ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { leader = { save_scope_as = no_borders_visionary } } | 这就是我们想要的未来！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = future_without_borders }ig:ig_intelligentsia ?= { leader = { add_modifier = { name = utopian_visionary days = normal_modifier_time } } }ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { add_modifier = { name = anti_border_rhetoric days = normal_modifier_time } } 精神可嘉，但似乎天真地过于乌托邦了。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = future_freer_movement } |
| 外来信仰 migration_laws.2 [current law] 的提议在虔信派中引发了关于移民是否会认同 [country-adj] 独特宗教情感的辩论。“即使他们是 [religion]，谁又能说他们不会带来什么奇怪的外国仪式？一种被故土民间习俗玷污的、掺了假的 [religion] 信仰。如果纵容这类事物，我们自己的传统将会如何？” | Migration Controls No Migration Controls | 辩论 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }ig:ig_devout ?= { NOT = { law_stance = { law = root.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } }NOT = { has_modifier = secular_immigration_policy }NOT = { has_modifier = considering_religious_immigration_restrictions }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_migration_religious_restrictions } } | ig:ig_devout ?= { save_scope_as = devout_ig } | 我们应当接受那些不按我们的方式践行信仰的移民。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = secular_immigration_policy }ig:ig_devout ?= { add_modifier = { name = faithless_hordes days = normal_modifier_time } } 也许我们可以设置一些限制来防止这种情况。 currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_migration_religious_restrictions sponsor = scope:devout_ig cooldown = 120 } }scope:devout_ig = { improve_law_stance = yes } |
| 外来竞争 migration_laws.3 生活在 [ improverished_state state name ] 的人们处境艰难，他们担心 [current law] 的改革会加剧就业市场的竞争。“这是谁的主意？大概是一个不必每天担心有没有活干、有没有钱挣、能不能活下去的人吧。富裕的移民对于那些已经衣食无忧的人来说，似乎从来都不是威胁。但我告诉你，在我们这些身处底层的人这里，从来都是拥挤不堪的。” | 迁移管制 无迁移管制 | 延宕 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls AND = { has_law = law_type:law_closed_borders is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_scope_state = { average_sol < 15 }NOT = { has_modifier = free_movement_of_labor }NOT = { has_modifier = welfare_for_all }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_closed_borders value > neutral } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_nativist_labour_market } } | random_scope_state = { limit = { average_sol < 15 } save_scope_as = improverished_state } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_closed_borders value > neutral } } save_scope_as = closed_borders_ig } | 如果他们竞争不过，丢了工作也是活该。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = free_movement_of_labor }scope:improverished_state = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = medium_radicals strata = lower } } 我们会确保所有人都能得到照顾，如果真到了那一步的话。 trigger = { has_law = law_type:law_old_age_pension }add_enactment_modifier = { name = welfare_for_all }every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_old_age_pension value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = welfare_for_the_world days = normal_modifier_time } } [Country-adj] 的求职者应当被优先考虑！ currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_nativist_labour_market sponsor = scope:closed_borders_ig cooldown = 60 } } |
| 把 [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c adjective ] 人挡在外面 migration_laws.4 一股针对 [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c name ] 邻国的敌意浪潮为推行 [current law] 的努力推波助澜，其主张是这项改革对于阻止来自该国的迁移是必要的。“当你说边境管制的时候，我们都知道你说的是哪条边境。你不用说出口。不过你倒是应该说。承认我们想把谁挡在外面，没什么好羞耻的。我们没有什么可羞耻的。该羞耻的是 #italic 他们#!” | 封闭边界 迁移管制 | 推进 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_neighbouring_state = { NOT = { owner = root } }NOT = { has_modifier = fear_neighbor_immigration }NOT = { has_modifier = fear_general_immigration } | random_neighbouring_state = { owner = { save_scope_as = immigrant_fear_neighbor } } | 那些 [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c adjective ] 猪猡正是这部法律出台的原因！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = fear_neighbor_immigration }change_relations = { country = scope:immigrant_fear_neighbor value = -30 } 这是一项普遍性的改革，与 [ immigrant_fear_neighbor c name ] 毫无关系。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = fear_general_immigration } |
| 人与商品 migration_laws.5 一些自由贸易的支持者担心，朝向 [current law] 的移民改革是将 [country] 与外部世界隔绝的第一步，不仅针对移民，也针对市场。“这些看似是彼此独立的问题和原则，但那只是一种肤浅的分析。我们正在向全世界传递一个信息：这里不欢迎你们。我们怎么可能一边排斥外国的人民，一边又指望他们心甘情愿地与我们分享他们所能给予的一切？” | 封闭边界 迁移管制 | 辩论 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }country_has_isolationism_or_sakoku = no NOT = { has_modifier = trade_restrictions_considered }NOT = { has_modifier = trade_restrictions_rejected }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } }NOT = { currently_enacting_law = { has_amendment = amendment_type:amendment_migration_tariff_exemptions } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_isolationism_ig } | 也许确实如此！ every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_isolationism value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = anti_free_trade_rhetoric days = normal_modifier_time } } 我们将在法律条文中明确表明并非如此。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = trade_restrictions_rejected }currently_enacting_law = { add_amendment = { type = amendment_migration_tariff_exemptions sponsor = scope:anti_isolationism_ig cooldown = 120 } } |
| [ migrant_pop culture name ] 人的恳求 migration_laws.6 为回应 [current law] 的提案，生活在 [ migrant_pop_state state name ] 的 [ migrant_pop culture name ] 人指出，他们自己当年也是移民到此，未来的限制措施可能会阻碍他们的亲属前来团聚。“我们所有人都是在某个时刻来到这里的。有的是昨天，有的是在久已被遗忘的年代。也许被遗忘是有原因的。当你们的人民占据你们如今称之为己有的土地时，流了多少血？你们哪里来的道德权威，来告诉我们谁有权加入我们这里？” | 封闭边界 迁移管制 | 延宕 | OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_closed_borders AND = { has_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls is_enacting_law = law_type:law_migration_controls } }any_scope_pop = { state = { save_temporary_scope_as = m_state } culture = { NOT = { has_homeland = scope:m_state } NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = root } } }NOT = { has_modifier = no_family_immigration }NOT = { has_modifier = family_reunification_discussion } | random_scope_pop = { limit = { state = { save_temporary_scope_as = m_state } culture = { NOT = { has_homeland = scope:m_state } NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = root } } } culture = { save_scope_as = migrant_pop } state = { save_scope_as = migrant_pop_state } } | 阻止所有人把家人接来本来就是目的的一部分！ add_radicals = { value = large_radicals culture = scope:migrant_pop }add_enactment_modifier = { name = no_family_immigration } 这根本与 [ migrant_pop culture name ] 人无关。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = family_reunification_discussion } |
| 民众拥戴 monarchy_law_events.1 首都的一场活动人士游行队伍中加入了士兵和平民，众人齐声呼唤君主制。[ monarchist_ig_leader_scope character name ] 抓住机会直接向聚集的人群发表演讲。“朋友们！国家在呼唤领导，呼唤正义，呼唤混乱的终结！如果你们想要稳定，如果你们想要国家的意志得到完全实现，那就让我们一起投票选出一位国王，从此你们再也不用投票了。” | 君主制 | 推进 | OR = { has_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic has_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic has_law = law_type:law_council_republic }NOT = { has_modifier = monarchist_agitation }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = monarchist_ig_scope leader = { save_scope_as = monarchist_ig_leader_scope } } | [ monarchist_ig_leader_scope character name ] 是一位真正精通 [his/her] 本行的人物。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_agitation }scope:monarchist_ig_leader_scope = { add_modifier = { name = the_man_of_the_hour days = normal_modifier_time } } [ monarchist_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 显然是君主主义者的家园，也因此是国家的家园。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_agitation }scope:monarchist_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = attracting_notables days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| 世俗的耳语 monarchy_law_events.2 据说 [Ruler] 曾随口对多个世俗政府发表过正面评价。有人怀疑 [he/she] 已经厌倦了以神权者的身份统治 [country]。“是的是的，我当然关心我羔羊们的灵魂。没有什么使命比拯救人类、救赎迷途者、让信仰获得胜利更崇高。但是……跟我说实话，戴上一顶王冠有什么不好呢？” | 君主制 | 推进 | has_law = law_type:law_theocracy any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } }NOT = { has_modifier = monarchist_agitation } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = monarchist_ig_scope } random_scope_character = { limit = { is_ruler_of_own_country = yes } save_scope_as = secularizing_spiritualist } | [ secularizing_spiritualist character name ] 很清楚风向往哪边吹。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_agitation }scope:secularizing_spiritualist = { add_modifier = { name = monarchist_spiritualist days = normal_modifier_time } } [ monarchist_ig_scope interestgroup name ] 很欣慰自己的想法得到认可。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_agitation }scope:monarchist_ig_scope = { add_modifier = { name = attracting_notables days = normal_modifier_time } } |
| 进口的蓝血贵族？ monarchy_law_events.3 关于究竟应由谁登上王位的问题，促使一些君主主义者提议邀请 [ neighboring_monarchy country adjective ] 王室的一位成员前来担任我们的君主。说得委婉些，这个想法引发了激烈争议。“其实很简单。我们需要一个人，不仅能象征我们国家的力量与稳定，而且已经深刻理解君主的期待与职责。还有谁比一个在王室宫廷中长大的人更适合扮演这个角色呢？” | 君主制 | 辩论 | any_interest_group = { is_in_government = no ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } }NOT = { has_variable = monarchy_law_debate_timer }any_neighbouring_state = { owner = { has_law = law_type:law_monarchy } } | random_neighbouring_state = { limit = { owner = { has_law = law_type:law_monarchy } } owner = { save_scope_as = neighboring_monarchy } } set_variable = { name = monarchy_law_debate_timer days = 730 # two years } | 我们还是别鼓励这些人发言了。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_debate } 每一位君主主义者的声音都应被倾听。 random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_debate_success } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_debate_fail } } } |
| 金箔王冠 monarchy_law_events.4 一些君主主义者一直在讨论王室御用品应当是什么样子，结果发现国家竟然没有任何真正适合君主的东西。一顶用锡和金箔自制的王冠被人抓住把柄，作为 Proposed 君主制“天生寒酸”本质的象征。“我们美丽的君主制——因为我们必须实行君主制——需要朴素而雅致的御用品所特有的那份庄重。为此，我已经联系了数位金匠、宝石匠、珠宝商，还有一家能制作像样王座的店铺，他们告诉我用的是上等的科林斯皮革。与此同时……” | 君主制 | 延宕 | any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } }NOT = { has_variable = monarchist_law_stall_timer } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value > neutral } } save_scope_as = book_burning_ig } set_variable = { name = monarchist_law_stall_timer days = 730 # two years } | 那顶王冠不过是个象征罢了！呃…… add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_stall } 那顶王冠就是象征！是国家对君主制之爱的象征！ random_list = { 1 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_stall_fail } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = monarchist_stall_success } } } |
| [Ruler] 缺席内阁会议 republic_laws.1 [Title] [ruler] 缺席了 [his/her] 政府的一次内阁会议，内阁成员们未能就如何推进达成任何结论。“如果我们的 [title] 连决定国家未来的内阁会议都不出席，我们还怎么能指望公民对政府抱有任何信心？显然，他和其他贵族外出狂欢，就在会议预定开始的几个小时前才上床睡觉。” | 议会共和制 总统共和制 | 推进 | has_law = law_type:law_monarchy or = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic }NOT = { has_modifier = republic_representation } |  | 这对我们 [title] 来说是不可接受的行为。 random_list = { 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = republic_representation multiplier = 4 } } 3 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = republic_representation } } } 也许他只是不小心睡过头了。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = republic_representation multiplier = 2 } 把它登在报刊上。 trigger = { or = { has_law = law_type:law_right_of_assembly has_law = law_type:law_protected_speech } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = republic_representation multiplier = 6 } |
| [title] 的命运 republic_laws.2 [ rulers_fate interestgroup name ] 的 [ monarchist_supporter character name ] 问道，如果政府变为 [current law]，[title] 将会有怎样的下场。“君主制是支撑这个国家的核心支柱之一。我们不能认真考虑直接将其废除的想法。”“如果我们打算让一个人凌驾于所有人之上，就不可能拥有一个公平平等的国家。如果他想保留他的贵族身份，我们就应该把他送到遥远的地方去。[title] 在我们的社会中已经没有容身之地了。” | 议会共和制 总统共和制 | 辩论 | has_law = law_type:law_monarchy or = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic is_enacting_law = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic }NOT = { has_modifier = fate_of_the_monarchy }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_monarchy value > neutral } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = law_type:law_monarchy value > neutral } } save_scope_as = rulers_fate leader = { save_scope_as = monarchist_supporter } } | 让 [title] 退位。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = fate_of_the_monarchy } 将 [title] 流放。 scope:rulers_fate = { add_modifier = { name = exile_the_monarch days = normal_modifier_time } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = fate_of_the_monarchy multiplier = 2 } |

| 划时代案件 rights_of_women_laws.1 在一起吸引了全国目光的诉讼案中，一名法官裁定家庭暴力构成离婚的正当理由。“仅仅因为摔碎一个盘子，他就一次又一次地殴打她。这只是从蜜月延续至今的一系列殴打中最近的一次。我们怎么能把任何女性交给这无休止的惩罚？让身处暴力处境中的女性离婚，不仅是公平，更是正义本身的体现。” | 妇女工作 已婚妇女财产权 | 推进 | any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } }NOR = { has_modifier = womens_rights_divorce_advance has_modifier = womens_rights_divorce_lesser_advance }AND = { OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_women_own_property is_enacting_law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace } OR = { has_law = law_type:law_no_womens_rights has_law = law_type:law_women_own_property } } | random_interest_group = { limit = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } save_scope_as = anti_women_ig } | 这极大地支持了我们的立场。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_rights_divorce_advance }scope:anti_women_ig = { add_modifier = { name = womens_rights_divorce_ig_anger days = normal_modifier_time } } 我们还是不要庆祝得太张扬，以免触怒 [ anti_women_ig interestgroup name ]。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_rights_divorce_lesser_advance } |

| 歇斯底里的论断 rights_of_women_laws.3 一篇近期发表的关于“女性歇斯底里症”的论文正在医学界获得越来越多的认可。这篇论文被用作反对 [current law] 法律的反女权宣传。“没有任何真实证据表明‘歇斯底里症’在女性中比在男性中更为普遍，但不知为何，被诊断出这种病的总是我们。” | 妇女工作 已婚妇女财产权 | 延宕 | NOR = { has_modifier = womens_hysteria_stall has_modifier = womens_hysteria_stall_fail has_modifier = womens_hysteria_stall_success }AND = { OR = { is_enacting_law = law_type:law_women_own_property is_enacting_law = law_type:law_women_in_the_workplace } OR = { has_law = law_type:law_no_womens_rights has_law = law_type:law_women_own_property } }any_interest_group = { ig_counts_as_marginal = no law_stance = { law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type value < neutral } } |  | 这很棘手。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_hysteria_stall } 也许我们可以自己做研究？ random_list = { 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_hysteria_stall_fail } } 2 = { add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_hysteria_stall_success multiplier = 0.5 } } } 花时间发布一份全面的驳斥。 trigger = { enactment_setback_count < max_setbacks_minus_one }add_enactment_setback = 1 add_enactment_modifier = { name = womens_hysteria_stall_success } |

| 街头沸腾 single_party_state.3 为谴责[ruling party]建立[current law]的举动，主要隶属于[ protester_ig interestgroup name ]的抗议者在[capital state]各城市发起了持续多日的街头占领。[capital city]的街道成了声音的海洋，口号随着组织者指挥的合唱此起彼伏。他们的声音在街道间回荡，穿透窗户，映照在警察胸前的警徽上，警察在他们面前排成了一道道人墙。今夜，[country]的街道生机勃勃——但在每位抗议者的眼中，还可以看到别的东西。冷酷的决心，以及对今夜或将 是最后一夜的恐惧。 | 一党制国家 | 停滞 | ruler.interest_group ?= { has_party = yes }any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_single_party_state value < neutral } } | ruler.interest_group.party = { save_scope_as = ruling_party } ruler.interest_group ?= { save_scope_as = loyal_ig } random_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_single_party_state value < neutral } } save_scope_as = protester_ig } capital = { save_scope_as = capital_scope } | 号召我们的忠诚派展示支持。 ai_chance = { base = 25 }scope:capital_scope = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = scope:protester_ig } add_loyalists_in_state = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = scope:loyal_ig } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_protests_burning_out }scope:loyal_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_reliance_on_support days = short_modifier_time } } 这种事情只需警棍，别的不必多谈。 ai_chance = { base = 25 modifier = { trigger = { scope:protester_ig = { ig_approval < -5 } } add = -15 } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_repressing_protests }scope:capital_scope = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = large_radicals interest_group = scope:protester_ig } }scope:protester_ig = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_vicious_repressions days = short_modifier_time } } 我们必须花更多时间向人民宣讲我们的制度。 ai_chance = { base = 25 }scope:capital_scope = { add_radicals_in_state = { value = small_radicals interest_group = scope:protester_ig } }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_peoples_tutelage } |
| 中止革命 single_party_state.4 [ruling party]清算[country]残余资产阶级政党的努力，遭到同情[ fascist_ig interestgroup name ]的极右翼势力的顽强阻挠。煤烟覆盖了[ruling party]总部的前墙，那是燃烧的油瓶砸向立面后爆发的短暂火海留下的沉积。民兵再次进入并封锁大楼时，碎玻璃在脚下嘎吱作响。显然，这绝非一起简单的破坏行为。这是对正在进行的革命的回应——一场针对无产阶级的战争行为。 | 一党制国家 | 停滞 | ruler.interest_group ?= { has_party = yes }OR = { ruler = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } ruler.interest_group.leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } }any_interest_group = { leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } } | ruler.interest_group.party = { save_scope_as = ruling_party } random_interest_group = { limit = { leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } } save_scope_as = fascist_ig } | 我们必须首先专注于自己的议程。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_fascists_hate_one_party_state } 趁法西斯分子还没藏起来，把他们撕碎！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_fascists_hate_one_party_state multiplier = 0.66 }every_interest_group = { limit = { leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_fascist_repressed } owner = { add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = prev } } } |
| 只为人民 single_party_state.5 [ruling party]巩固其对[country]统治的努力，遭到同情[ socialist_ig interestgroup name ]的社会主义势力的顽强阻挠。“我们绝不能接受这种对社会中反动势力的鲁莽纵容！无产阶级政党的任务，就是把劳动人民团结起来，组成整个无产阶级的统一战线。来吧，工人、农民、热爱自由与民主的人们……站出来反抗法西斯浪潮，拯救你们自己！”撕破的海报在泥泞水洼中只显现了一瞬，便又被一只靴子踩进了水面之下。 | 一党制国家 | 停滞 | ruler.interest_group ?= { has_party = yes }OR = { ruler = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } ruler.interest_group.leader = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } }any_interest_group = { leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } } | ruler.interest_group.party = { save_scope_as = ruling_party } random_interest_group = { limit = { leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } } save_scope_as = socialist_ig } | 国家的道路只属于我们自己。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_communists_hate_one_party_state } 不惜一切代价镇压共产主义者！ add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_communists_hate_one_party_state multiplier = 0.66 }every_interest_group = { limit = { leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_fascist_repressed } owner = { add_radicals = { value = medium_radicals interest_group = prev } } } |
| 以革命推行改革 single_party_state.6 由于[ruling party]的行动使许多国家事务陷入停摆，由[ communist_leader_scope character name ]指挥的共产主义民兵已占领[capital state]各地的重要建筑，宣告“资产阶级专政”的终结。我们的任务是破旧立新，建立一种更高级的民主形式，它将唤起被剥削的无产阶级大众去创造新的历史。凡雇佣他人牟利者；凡不靠自己劳动而靠资本利息、工业企业或地产收入为生者；私营商人、代理人、中间商；各宗派的僧侣和祭司；旧警察机构的离职人员；精神病患者与罪犯……所有这些人都不得进入人民的政党。 | 一党制国家 | 推进 | ruler.interest_group ?= { has_party = yes }OR = { ruler = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } ruler.interest_group.leader = { has_socialist_ideology = yes } } | ruler.interest_group.party = { save_scope_as = ruling_party } ruler.interest_group.leader = { save_scope_as = communist_leader_scope } capital = { save_scope_as = capital_scope } | 一切权力归于无产阶级！ ai_chance = { base = 25 modifier = { trigger = { any_interest_group = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_single_party_state value < neutral } is_powerful = yes } } add = -15 } }activate_law = law_type:law_single_party_state every_interest_group = { limit = { law_stance = { law = law_type:law_single_party_state value < neutral } } add_modifier = { name = modifier_death_of_democracy days = long_modifier_time is_decaying = yes } }every_interest_group = { limit = { has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist } add_modifier = { name = modifier_death_of_nation days = long_modifier_time is_decaying = yes } }add_radicals = { value = large_radicals } 阻止这场迫在眉睫的政变！ ai_chance = { base = 15 }add_radicals = { value = very_large_radicals interest_group = ruler.interest_group }add_enactment_modifier = { name = modifier_revolution_averted }ruler = { add_modifier = { name = modifier_betrayed_revolution days = normal_modifier_time } } |

| 大众的鸦片 state_atheism.1 在我们通过[current law]法律之际，有人担心没有了[religion]信仰提供精神寄托，困苦的民众可能会陷入绝望。宗教是被压迫生灵的叹息，是无情世界的心脏，是没有灵魂的环境中的灵魂。它是人民的鸦片。 | 国家无神论 | 辩论 |  |  | 上帝已死。 add_enactment_modifier = { name = opium_of_the_masses_enactment } 我们要填饱他们的肚子，而不是滋养他们的灵魂。 add_modifier = { name = opium_of_the_masses_mod days = long_modifier_time } 鸦片才是大众的鸦片。 trigger = { market = { mg:opium = { market_goods_consumption > 100 } } any_primary_culture = { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = opium } } }random_primary_culture = { limit = { NOT = { has_cultural_obsession = opium } } add_cultural_obsession = opium } |
